# Ladder v2 — results report (A1 | A2 | A3)

**What ladder v2 is.** The whole experimental ladder (E0–E26, as enumerated in `docs/ladder_v2/manifest.yaml`) re-run, entry by entry through one runner (`artifacts/ladder_v2_run.py`), on three encoders pretrained on `pretrain_v2` with seeds 0, 1 and 2, all reading one identical probe split (`LADDER_SPLIT: identical`), one shared K = 30 untrained bank (`shared/p1_untrained_bank.json`, built once) and the frozen effect floor 0.7267. Each encoder has its own output directory (`runs/ladder_v2/a1`, `a2`, `a3`); entries that do not depend on the encoder are in `shared/` or built once on A1 and linked.

| encoder | seed | checkpoint | SHA-1 |
|---|---|---|---|
| A1 | 0 | `runs/m_v2/encoder.pt` | `34bb30a74d38b1fc4622861f43959a92fc01520c` |
| A2 | 1 | `runs/m_v2_s1/encoder.pt` | `380cdb7b67a97873c7b2c3b0458a638966eff9b1` |
| A3 | 2 | `runs/m_v2_s2/encoder.pt` | `4c3e1c7e88f662a40cae07141b859db264ddac0a` |

Checkpoint SHA-1s are as given in the brief; this report did not open or re-hash the checkpoints (they lie outside `runs/ladder_v2`). The audit hashed them as pins and reports all pins unmoved.

- **HEAD:** `4bc25a60bfec13ba6d5ba25fd2dd894b812d56fd` (branch `ladder-v2`).
- **Audit verdict** (`docs/ladder_v2/audit.md`, SHA-1 `64dc5b3cb984ff5a159752eb983a3dbffae792fa`): **COMPLETE** — 205/205 entries `done` (exit 0), 0 failed, 0 skipped; 432/432 recorded outputs re-hashed and matching both records; 53/53 distinct pins (2958 pin references) unmoved.
- **Run records:** `manifest.yaml` `6c2f462009ce`, `state.json` `16b521906ec9`, `outputs.sha1` `26989f55eb50`, `run_log.md` `c2ab5e19a6d8` (SHA-1, first 12 hex).
- **Blinding followed.** Written from the result files alone: outputs under `runs/ladder_v2/{a1,a2,a3,shared}`; the run records (`manifest.yaml`, `state.json`, `outputs.sha1`, `run_log.md`, `audit.md`) and `inventory.md`, `plan.md`, `build_notes.md`; driver and package source only to learn what an output field or state means (prose in drivers about earlier results on another encoder was disregarded); `configs/probe.yaml`; and the `value` field of `configs/effect_floor.json`. Not read: any `*_findings.md`, `artifacts/aligned_comparison.md`, `artifacts/out/`, anything under `runs/` other than `runs/ladder_v2`, the entry logs in `runs/ladder_v2/logs/`, the project plan/TODO/spec/design notes named in the brief, user memory, or `.env`. No experiment was run and no file other than this report was written. No comparison is made with any earlier encoder or result; the three encoders are reported side by side only.
- **Conventions.** Every per-encoder value is that encoder's own; nothing is averaged or pooled across seeds. Shared entries are reported once. Agreement: `3/3` (all three states equal), `2/1 (A? differs)`, `1/1/1 (all differ)`, or `—` (no state). Where an output carries no state, its statistic is given with `—`. A file is cited as `path` (`first 12 hex of its SHA-1`), paths relative to `runs/ladder_v2/`. Values are as recorded, rounded for display only.
- **Field notes (ambiguities).** (i) `h5_probes.jsonl` (E0) and `o1_matched.json` (E3) carry `"smoke": true` as a hard-coded literal in their drivers, not `ProbingConfig.smoke`; it does not mark these entries as smoke runs, but the field as written is ambiguous. (ii) Several records name the encoder under test with the key `M` (or `auc_M`, `m_…`, `auc_m`); this report displays those as "trained"/"encoder under test". (iii) The existence p-value of E5 is not recorded in any E5 file; only `exceeds_null` is. (iv) E8 (S1) records no state. (v) The E24 width note (see E24).

## Front matrix — answers × per-answer experiments
Each cell is the state as `A1/A2/A3`, followed in its own column by agreement across the three encoders (`3/3`, `2/1 (A? differs)`, `1/1/1 (all differ)`). The detail, statistics, CIs and nulls are in the per-experiment sections below; this matrix only places the states side by side. Abbreviations: `ng` = not gated (the answer is not R1/R2 in that encoder's own E5 record, so the experiment does not test it there); `nuf` = no usable floor; UNRES. = UNRESOLVED; COLL. = COLLAPSES; SURV. = SURVIVES; PART. = PARTIAL; DEGEN. = DEGENERATE; straight = straight_within_noise; no traj. = no_trajectory; uncharact. = uncharacterised_occupancy; NO EV. = NO EVIDENCE; EV. = EVIDENCE; SENS. C = SENSITIVE TO C; BEYOND VIS. = BEYOND VISIBILITY; RES.-LIM. = RESOLUTION-LIMITED; UNCHAR. = UNCHARACTERISED; UNREM. = UNREMARKABLE; SUBM. = SUBMERGED; (negl.) = (negligible); ENC. AXIS / PART. AXIS = ENCODER AXIS / PARTIAL AXIS; NOT>UNTR. = NOT ABOVE UNTRAINED; +NP = "— NUISANCE PROXY"; SMALL N / LARGE N = LARGEST AT SMALL N / LARGE N; INSUFF. = INSUFFICIENT; UNCH. / IMPR. / WORS. = UNCHANGED / IMPROVED / WORSENED. E6 (pairs), E8, E10, E12–E14, E16–E20, E22, E24 and E26 are not per answer and appear only in their own sections.

## Per experiment

### E0 — Headline single-feature probe
What it tests: a logistic probe (C = 1) on frozen embeddings predicting the featured-or-disk consensus (`t01_smooth_or_features_a02_features_or_disk_fraction`, extremes ≤ 0.2 vs ≥ 0.8) on the identical split (train capped at 40,000 by stride; test uncapped), via `artifacts/h5_probe_lean.py`. The record also scores the same probe on the whole test set and on the ambiguous middle (0.2 < fraction < 0.8). Criterion: none in the driver — it reports the AUC with a bootstrap 95% CI; there is no null or floor in this record, so state and agreement are `—`.

| item | A1 | A2 | A3 | agreement |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| consensus extremes (headline) | AUC 0.9571 [0.9541, 0.9603]; n_test 21,973 | AUC 0.9701 [0.9677, 0.9725]; n_test 21,973 | AUC 0.9591 [0.9561, 0.9621]; n_test 21,973 | — |
| whole test set | AUC 0.8723 [0.8685, 0.8765]; n_test 34,828 | AUC 0.8898 [0.8863, 0.8936]; n_test 34,828 | AUC 0.8749 [0.8711, 0.8790]; n_test 34,828 | — |
| ambiguous middle | AUC 0.6865 [0.6768, 0.6956]; n_test 12,855 | AUC 0.7004 [0.6914, 0.7099]; n_test 12,855 | AUC 0.6902 [0.6809, 0.6998]; n_test 12,855 | — |
| n_train (extremes) / train pool | 25,304 / 39,997 | 25,304 / 39,997 | 25,304 / 39,997 | — |

Source: A1 `a1/h5_probes.jsonl` (`9ec4050e5583`); A2 `a2/h5_probes.jsonl` (`68e260816004`); A3 `a3/h5_probes.jsonl` (`10a8b6a6ba4f`)

Field note: every record carries `"smoke": true`. In `h5_probe_lean.py` this value is a hard-coded literal written into every record (it is not `ProbingConfig.smoke`, and `configs/probe.yaml` has `smoke: false`); its intended meaning is not documented in the driver, so it is reported as found. The `runs` field is the driver's default directory and is not where the checkpoint came from (the `checkpoint` field gives the encoder used).

### E1 — Full-split embedding bank
What it builds: block-11 mean-pooled embeddings of every galaxy in the identical split's uncapped union, used by E2 and E26 (`artifacts/ladder_v2_c2.py` links an existing bank only if its checkpoint SHA-1 matches the encoder and its ids equal the union; otherwise it re-embeds). Criterion: the bank's recorded checkpoint SHA-1 must equal the encoder's pin. No statistic.

| item | A1 | A2 | A3 | agreement |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| shape (ids × dims) | 195,677 × 384 | 195,677 × 384 | 195,677 × 384 | — |
| recorded checkpoint | `runs/m_v2/encoder.pt` | `runs/m_v2_s1/encoder.pt` | `runs/m_v2_s2/encoder.pt` | — |
| recorded checkpoint SHA-1 = manifest pin | yes (`34bb30a74d38`) | yes (`380cdb7b67a9`) | yes (`4c3e1c7e88f6`) | — |

Source: A1 `a1/c2_a1_embeddings.npz` (`c4eb12f74402`); A2 `a2/c2_a2_embeddings.npz` (`1042f605ba48`); A3 `a3/c2_a3_embeddings.npz` (`7341f6fbcadc`) (symlinks; the SHA-1 is of the resolved target, as the runner records it).

### E2 — Per-answer AUC, 37 answers, full identical split
What it tests: for each of the 37 Scheme-1 answers, a logistic probe (C = 1) fitted on the E1 bank over the full identical train split and scored on the 34,828-galaxy test split, with a 95% interval from 2,000 Poisson galaxy-bootstrap draws (seed 20260926), via `artifacts/ladder_v2_e2.py` (protocol of `aligned_c2`). Criterion: none — the ladder v2 driver applies no powered filter, no multiplicity correction and no null; state and agreement are therefore `—`. Cells: AUC [95% CI]; n_pos/n_test.

### E3 — Matched evaluation (O1)
What it tests: six features from `SPREAD`, each probed unmatched and then on test/train sets matched on one nuisance (magnitude, size, SNR, redshift, PSF; 5 quantile strata, class-balanced) or jointly on magnitude × size, against an untrained-encoder (seed 0) null fitted on the same rows. Criterion (O1, recorded in the file's `read` field): SURVIVES if (M − C_m) ≥ 0.5·(A − C) and M's CI lower bound > C_m; COLLAPSES if (M − C_m) ≤ 0 or M's CI contains C_m; PARTIAL between; UNRESOLVED if < 500 matched test galaxies or < 10% of the unmatched test set. Cells: verdict; matched AUC M [95% CI] vs matched null C_m; retained fraction; matched n_test. Unmatched rows: A [CI] vs untrained null C.

Feature roles (as recorded): `t01_smooth_or_features_a02_features_or_disk`: clean binary — the comparison point; `t02_edgeon_a04_yes`: clean binary — strong visual signal; `t10_arms_winding_a28_tight`: graded axis (1/3) — ordered question; `t10_arms_winding_a29_medium`: graded axis (2/3); `t10_arms_winding_a30_loose`: graded axis (3/3); `t09_bulge_shape_a26_boxy`: deep + confused — 89.8% of positives on <=2 votes.

Split: n_train 39,997, n_test 34,828 (all three encoders).

### E4 — Untrained bank, K = 30 (shared)
What it builds: for 30 random-initialisation ViTs of the same architecture, the 37 Scheme-1 AUCs on the identical split (`artifacts/p1_untrained_bank.py --seeds 30`), keyed on a model-config hash, a split hash and the pixel-pipeline hash. This is D23's bar for E5's existence test; it does not depend on the trained encoder, so it is reported once. The file stores only the 30 per-seed AUCs. **Derived here** (not fields of the file), exactly as `nulls.untrained_z_pvalue` forms them: the mean and the sample SD (ddof = 1) over the 30 seeds; min and max are given for range. The Shapiro–Wilk p is D23's normality check as recorded by E5's `p2_ladder.json` (`normality`), identical in all three encoders' records because the bank is shared.

Keys: model `c44bdef1dbdf5acc`, split `da12ce53250b814d`, pixels `246d8de6ba9a…`; max_train 40,000; seeds 30. Normality records identical across A1/A2/A3: yes.

### E5 — The existence ladder, Scheme 1, full and conditional populations (with E6 inside)
What it tests (`probing/ladder.run_ladder`, driven by `artifacts/t1_ladder.py`, the production record; `p2_ladder.py` and `s2_verify.py` are earlier drivers of the same `run_ladder`, run as their own entries because later stages read them): per answer, an L2 logistic probe (C = 1) on the 39,997-galaxy capped train split, scored on the 34,828 test split, with a bootstrap SE (2,000 draws). **Existence** (D23 `untrained_z`): z = (AUC − mean C)/√(sd C² + SE²) against the K = 30 untrained bank (E4), one-sided Student t with df = 29, Benjamini–Yekutieli at α = 0.05 over the 37 answers → `exceeds_null`; `clean` additionally requires AUC ≥ the frozen effect floor 0.7267 (`configs/effect_floor.json` value). **Rung** (from `ladder._passing_rung` / `_failing_rung`): an existence-passing answer is **R1** only if it exceeds the null, is clean, has selectivity ≥ 0.10, clears nuisances and is not entangled; otherwise **R2**, with the mechanism naming the gate that failed (clearance not passed → "confounded by …"/"nuisance clearance unresolved"; entangled → "entangled linear"; not clean → "below effect floor"; else "not selective"). An existence-failing answer goes to the MLP capacity sweep and is **R4** "MLP decode unadjudicated" (D25: no untrained-MLP bar exists, so R3 cannot be assigned). **Nuisance clearance** (D24): the worst nuisance is always matched; retention verdict by O1's rule (see E3) with C and C_m averaged over 3 untrained draws (`k_bar` = 3); an answer with no competitive nuisance clears regardless of the matched verdict, and an answer without an established margin is UNRESOLVED by construction. `resolvable_margin` is the smallest margin over the bar the answer could show at 80% power (BY rank-m threshold); `underpowered` is set when that margin exceeds 0.05 (`nulls.UNDERPOWERED_MARGIN`). The existence p-value itself is not written to any E5 record; only its outcome (`exceeds_null`) is.

The item column gives each answer's shared null: the K = 30 untrained bar mean ± SD (derived from `shared/p1_untrained_bank.json`, see E4) and the floor 0.7267.

#### E5 full population — rung (existence + rung gates)
Cell: **rung**; exceeds_null / clean; AUC ± bootstrap SE; resolvable margin (underpowered flag); positives/n_test; mechanism.

#### E5 — the three E5 records agree

### E6 — Entanglement: Marchenko–Pastur, probe-direction geometry, pair adjudication (inside E5)
What it tests (`probing/entanglement.py`, run inside `run_ladder`): over the unit probe directions of the existence-passing answers, the cosine and Gram matrices, the Gram effective rank against the embedding covariance's effective rank (span ratio), one global Marchenko–Pastur upper-edge test (`mp_method: upper_edge`; Tracy–Widom is not implemented), the logistic-vs-CAV disagreement, and for the top 10% of pairs by |cos| (`entangled_pair_quantile` 0.90) a D25 retention match of A on B's vote fraction. Criterion (`entanglement.adjudicate_pair`, fixed): **representational_entanglement** if MP significant, survived matching, |cos| ≥ 0.30 and logistic/CAV disagreement ≥ 0.10; **world_correlation** if MP significant and the matched direction does not survive (retention COLLAPSES); **clean** if MP not significant, no CAV disagreement and |cos| < 0.30; otherwise **inconclusive** (PARTIAL/UNRESOLVED retention gives `survived_matching = None`). Also recorded: Spearman between the embedding cosine matrix and the human vote-correlation matrix on the same galaxies, and D13 stage 2 (`bar_winding_alignment`) on the bar direction vs the winding answers. The global geometry, human-structure and bar×winding fields exist only in `p2_ladder.json` (full population); the pair verdicts of the production record are `t1_ladder.json` `full_pairs` / `conditional_pairs`.

The bar × winding criterion (from `bar_winding_alignment`, as stated in its recorded `reason`): `physics_consistent` when loose > tight (the ordering Hart et al. predict); `uniform_bleed` when the bar direction sits within a small spread of every winding answer; medium winding is absent from the comparison where it failed existence.

### E7 — Brief R: nonlinear structure and geometry (`artifacts/r_nonlinear.py`, `artifacts/r2_reach_split.py`)
Descriptive by the driver's own statement ("nothing here changes a rung"). Stages, in the order run: **r0**, **r3**, **r2**, **r1** (build notes: r0 first because S2 reads it), then the reach split. The untrained seeds 1–2 bank (`r_untrained_seeds.npz`) is built once on A1 and linked for A2/A3; seed 0 is each encoder's O1 bank.

#### E7 r0 — retention re-adjudication on the worst nuisance (all answers, both populations)
Criterion: O1's retention rule (see E3) on the worst nuisance, C and C_m the mean of three untrained draws; unlike E5's D24 clearance, r0 applies no margin floor, so an existence-failing answer can still receive a verdict. DEGENERATE = a one-class split. Cell: **verdict**; A [95% CI] vs C; M (M_lo) vs C_m; retained; nuisance; matched n_test.

#### E7 r3 — orientation circle: validation of the R2 instrument
Criterion: on galaxies with `expAB_r` ≤ 0.6 and stamp-moment q ≤ 0.8, R2's path machinery is run on E[z | position angle] (12 bins). "curved" = curvature p ≤ 0.05 and bend (1 − straightness) ≥ 0.10; a circle is **recovered** iff a path exists, it is curved, effective dimension ≥ 1.5, the centroids advance in order with at most one violation, and the path closes. `instrument_validated` = the circle is recovered on the trained encoder. Per the driver, if the instrument is not validated, R2's "straight" verdicts are uninterpretable (not evidence for feature = direction). Ridge R² on the doubled angle is descriptive.

#### E7 r2 — centroid path E[z | vote fraction]: straight, curved or tangled
Criterion (`geometry.curvature_verdicts`): over 10 vote-fraction bins, split-half cross-fitted; **curved** iff BY-significant (α = 0.05, over the answers whose path exists) against the shuffled-value null (200 draws, topped up to 4,000 when p is below the loosest BY threshold) **and** bend = 1 − straightness ≥ 0.10; otherwise **straight_within_noise**; **no_trajectory** if no path exists; **uncharacterised_occupancy** if too few bins are occupied. `ordered` = monotonicity (|Spearman| of bin order along the best-fit line) ≥ 0.9. Bins need ≥ 100 galaxies. Record-level: A1 instrument_validated False, family 36, expected false curved at 5% 1.80, topped up 29, top-up threshold 0.01190; A2 instrument_validated False, family 36, expected false curved at 5% 1.80, topped up 32, top-up threshold 0.01190; A3 instrument_validated False, family 36, expected false curved at 5% 1.80, topped up 26, top-up threshold 0.01190. Because r3 did not validate the instrument in any encoder (above), the driver's rule makes "straight_within_noise" verdicts uninterpretable here; they are reported as recorded.

Cell: **verdict**; bend; p_curved (null draws); BY-significant; ordered.

### E8 — S1: spiral bend direction vs inclination (`artifacts/s1_spiral_inclination.py`)
What it tests: for `t04_spiral_a08_spiral`, the bend direction (second eigenvector of R2's cross-fitted path covariance, fitted on train) projected on test galaxies; partial correlation with `expAB_r` after removing 20 vote-fraction bin means from both; 2,000-draw bootstrap CI; shuffled-`expAB_r` null with 10,000 permutations (two-sided add-one p); the share of the U-amplitude (middle-bin minus end-bin means) explained by inclination's within-fraction slope; full population and the well-voted half (question reach ≥ median); trained encoder and three untrained draws. Criterion: none in the record or the driver — its pre-registration lives in a findings file outside this report's sources, and the record's `prereg_sha` is null. State and agreement are therefore `—`. Cell: partial r [95% CI]; p_perm (null SD); raw r; share explained; n.

### E10 — U1: bar × winding / bar × arm-count cosines (`artifacts/u1_bar_winding.py`)
What it tests: the cosine between the bar probe direction (`t03_bar_a06_bar`) and each winding / arm-count answer's direction, against the human pairwise-complete vote correlation on the same galaxies. Null band = 2.576 × max(isotropic SD 1/√384, median SD of 30 shuffled-partner fits) (99%). Per-pair class (`classify`): |human| ≥ 0.30 is "strong"; |cos| > band is "outside"; strong & inside → HUMAN-ONLY; strong & outside → SHARED (same sign) or CONTRARY; not strong → ENCODER-ONLY (outside) or NEITHER. Overall verdict over the human-strong pairs: all HUMAN-ONLY → SUPPORTED (labelling bleed); all SHARED → REJECTED (the encoder carries the human association); none → UNTESTABLE; else MIXED. Untrained cosines are descriptive.

### E11 — U2: uncertainty geometry (`artifacts/u2_uncertainty.py --counts`, then default)
Targets are derived mechanically from each encoder's own E5 output: the answers that are R1 or R2 in that encoder's `t1_ladder.json`, per population (`gated_features`); the driver's hard-coded `FLAGGED` list is never read. Local floor on the question's reach: headline 21, fallback 10 if 21 is not usable, sensitivity 10 and 37 (usable: test middle ≥ N_MIN and ≥ 50 train extremes per class). Measurements at the headline floor: **on-axis** — logistic on train extremes (≥ 0.8 vs ≤ 0.2), projected on the test middle (0.2–0.8), Spearman with the vote fraction; **bend** (off-axis, primary) — S1's bend coordinate vs ambiguity a = 1 − 2|f − 0.5|; **line** (off-axis, secondary) — distance from the train-extreme centroid line vs ambiguity; each raw and as a Freedman–Lane partial controlling magnitude, SNR, size, redshift. 10,000 permutations; BY at α = 0.05 within each (population, measurement) family. States (`verdicts`): on-axis **TRACKS** (BY-significant, ρ > 0), **CONTRARY** (significant, ρ ≤ 0), **NO EVIDENCE**; bend/line **REVERSES** (significant partial of negative sign; D27 state, active in this run: `REVERSAL_STATE = True`), **NONE** (raw not significant-positive), **BEYOND VISIBILITY** (partial significant-positive), **RESOLUTION-LIMITED** (|partial| ≤ 0.5·|raw on the visibility rows|), else **UNRESOLVED**; **UNCHARACTERISED** if the bend direction could not be fitted. Untrained matrices are descriptive (no p). "not gated (R4)" = the answer is not R1/R2 in that encoder, so U2 does not test it there.

N_MIN (test-middle minimum) / min extremes per class: A1 483 / 50; A2 483 / 50; A3 483 / 50.

### E12 — U3: graded existence, Scheme 2 / D26 (`artifacts/u3_graded.py`)
What it tests: four ordered questions (arm winding, bulge prominence, roundedness, arm number); a galaxy's category is its plurality answer, question reach ≥ 21. **Test A**: logistic axis on train endpoint categories; test galaxies of every category projected; Jonckheere–Terpstra ordering as Kendall τ-b, one-sided permutation (10,000, add-one); BY over the 4 axes; adjacent-pair AUCs; untrained τ-b on three draws. Verdict: **ORDERED** if BY-significant and every adjacent AUC > 0.5 and τ-b > the largest untrained τ-b; **PARTLY ORDERED** if significant and above untrained (not all adjacent > 0.5); **NOT ABOVE UNTRAINED** if significant only; else **NOT ORDERED**. **Test B** (Masters et al. 2019 w_avg / B_avg; winding and bulge only): Spearman with the projection, permutation p; no state. **Test C** (where the middle sits): cross-fitted position t along the endpoint line (equal spacing expectation shown), off-line share, cosine of the off-line offset with the visibility pattern vs a 200-draw shuffled-category null (1%/99% quantiles), and Δ visibility index vs interpolation with a 1,000-draw bootstrap CI; no state.

Reach floor: A1 21; A2 21; A3 21.

### E14 — V2: vote-independent measurements (`artifacts/v2_independent.py`)
What it tests: **decode** — ridge (α by GCV) from the z-scored embedding to a measurement; test Spearman with bootstrap CI and permutation p; the same on three untrained draws; margin = ρ − max/min untrained. State (`decode_state`): **NOT DECODED** if not significant; **NEGLIGIBLE** if ρ < 0.10; **DECODED** if the margin over the largest untrained ρ is > 0; else **NOT ABOVE UNTRAINED**. Significance: BY over the confirmatory family of 5 (expAB_r decode, B/T decode, B_avg decode, A_m, A_v, primary block); `deVAB_r` and the unflagged block use p < 0.05. **Direction (2a)**: the ladder's logistic probe (and the CAV) on a vote label vs on a base-rate-matched axis-ratio label on the same galaxies; split-half reliabilities, r_raw, disattenuated r_d. State: **UNRELIABLE** (a reliability < 0.3), **OPPOSED** (r_raw < −band), **UNRELATED** (|r_raw| ≤ band = 2.576/√384), **SAME DIRECTION** (r_d ≥ 0.8), **RELATED** (r_d ≥ 0.3), else **DISTINCT**. **2×2 (2c, Simard B/T vs B_avg)**: partial Spearman A_m (B/T prediction vs B/T given B_avg) and A_v (B_avg prediction vs B_avg given B/T); each leg passes if significant, ρ ≥ 0.10 and > max untrained; verdict **BOTH** / **MEASUREMENT BEYOND VOTES** / **VOTES BEYOND MEASUREMENT** / **SHARED ONLY** (neither leg, both decodes DECODED) / **NEITHER** / **INVERTED** (a significant negative leg).

### E15 — V3: the encoder's own directions (`artifacts/v3_own_directions.py`)
What it tests (on the 39,997 train galaxies = A, the 34,828 test galaxies = B): covariance PCA on A; participation ratio PR, k* = round(PR); split-half stability. **Salience**: CAV energy of each answer in the top-k* subspace against 10,000 shuffled-label CAVs, BY over the gated answers (the answers R1/R2 in that encoder's own E5 full population, `gated_features`). State: **SALIENT** (significant, energy > null median), **SUBMERGED** (significant, energy ≤ null median), **UNREMARKABLE** (not significant); "(negligible)" appended when significant with ratio 0.9–1.1; **INSUFFICIENT** if a class has < 50. Answers outside the gated set carry statistics but no verdict ("not gated"). **Held-out axis**: one PC (of 50) + sign per gated answer chosen on A and frozen to `v3_alignment.json`, then AUC on B with a permutation p (BY over gated); recovery = (AUC_pc − 0.5)/(AUC_probe − 0.5). State: **INVERTED** (significant, AUC < 0.5), **NO AXIS** (not significant), **NOT ABOVE UNTRAINED** (recovery ≤ max untrained recovery), **ENCODER AXIS** (≥ 0.8), **PARTIAL AXIS** (≥ 0.5), else **WEAK AXIS**; "— NUISANCE PROXY" appended if the PC's |ρ| with any nuisance ≥ 0.5. **Discovery**: stable top-20 PCs matching no vote fraction (|ρ| < 0.3) classified ORIENTATION / NUISANCE / UNEXPLAINED (|ρ| ≥ 0.3 rule), with image grids.

### E16 — W2: naming PC1 and PC2 (`artifacts/w2_name_pcs.py --measure / --planted / --run`)
What it tests: covariance PCA on train as in V3; Spearman (10,000 permutations) of PC1 and PC2 scores over the union against stamp-measured candidates — confirmatory: padding fraction/status, interior invalid, sky g/r/i, noise g/r/i, crowding count, contaminating flux, g−r, u−r (BY over 26 = 13 × 2); exploratory: stamp colour, centroid offset, stamp flux, target area (p < 0.05). State (`state`): **NONE** if not significant; else |ρ| ≥ 0.5 **NAMES**, ≥ 0.3 **CONTRIBUTES**, ≥ 0.1 **WEAK**, else **NEGLIGIBLE** ("(exploratory)" appended for exploratory candidates). Joint: 5-fold CV R² of each PC on all confirmatory candidates (linear and gradient-boosted trees); **EXPLAINED** (trees R² ≥ 0.5), **PARTLY EXPLAINED** (≥ 0.2), else **UNEXPLAINED**. `vs_untrained`: the PC's best |ρ| with the untrained draws' top-10 PCs (descriptive). D28 planted checks precede the run (`w2_planted.json`).

### E17 — X1: handedness / pose symmetry (`artifacts/x1_handedness.py --bank / --planted / --run`)
What it tests: **X1a** — PC1, PC2 and the PC radius against spirality magnitude and signed variables (spiral and featured groups), with bimodality calls (BIMODAL: two modes either side of 0 with a dip ≤ 0.8 × the lower mode in ≥ 90% of 200 resamples; UNIMODAL CENTRED: one mode within 0.5 SD in ≥ 90%; else NEITHER). State (first match): INSUFFICIENT (a group < 300) → SIGNED TRACKS (a significant signed |ρ| ≥ 0.1) → CONSISTENT WITH HANDEDNESS (magnitude tracks, spirals bimodal, smooth centred) → MAGNITUDE ONLY → INVERSE MAGNITUDE → NO ASSOCIATION. **X1b** — GZ1 handedness votes vs PC1, PC2 and a plane fitted on half A, tested on half B (BY over 3): SIGNED ASSOCIATION (significant, |ρ| ≥ 0.1) / WEAK SIGNED ASSOCIATION / NO ASSOCIATION; a pixel chirality χ against the GZ1 votes gives the parity check: MIRROR CONFIRMED (p < 0.05, ρ > 0) / MIRROR CONTRADICTED (p < 0.05, ρ ≤ 0) / UNRESOLVED. **X1c** — 2,000 galaxies (1,000 spiral, 1,000 smooth) under rot90, rot180 and mirror: per component r_rot = min(r90, r180), r_mir, odd fraction, spiral concentration; state: r_rot ≤ 0.5 ORIENTATION-LIKE; < 0.8 ROTATION-SENSITIVE (WEAK); r_mir ≤ −0.5 HANDEDNESS (conc ≥ 2) or REFLECTION-ODD, NOT SPIRAL-CONCENTRATED; r_mir ≥ 0.8 INVARIANT TO BOTH; else PARTLY REFLECTION-SENSITIVE. Qualifier: LEARNED if r_mir is below every untrained draw's top-10 minimum, else ARCHITECTURAL-LEVEL. **Overall verdict** (first match): INSUFFICIENT → HANDEDNESS (any of PC1, PC2, plane-odd) → ORIENTATION-LIKE (PC1 and PC2) → INVARIANT TO BOTH (PC1 and PC2) → MIXED.

Table built mechanically: one row per state-bearing field of the record (path · field), the state per encoder, and — on the first state row of each path — the numeric fields recorded alongside it at the same level. "absent" = the path does not exist in that encoder's record. The path component `trained` is the record's fixed key for the encoder under test.

### E18 — Y3: pitch angle (`artifacts/y3_science.py --bank / --planted3 / --y3`)
What it tests, against machine-measured pitch (Hayes SpArcFiRe primary; Hart and Yu & Ho replications), BY within each family: **T1** agreement of GZ2 w_avg with pitch (no embeddings): INSUFFICIENT / NO AGREEMENT / CONTRARY (ρ > 0) / AGREE (ρ ≤ −0.3 and partial ≤ −0.3) / WEAK AGREEMENT. **T2** the 2×2 on Hayes (pitch decode vs w_avg decode; A_m pitch beyond votes, A_v votes beyond pitch): V2's decode states, and V2's 2×2 states with each leg additionally required to exceed the shared-quantity null's 97.5th percentile (`state`; `state_y3_rule` uses the ridge null at the 95th). **T2 replication** (Hayes decoder transferred; 5-fold within reference): NOT REPLICATED / INVERTED / REPLICATED / REPLICATED, NOT ABOVE UNTRAINED. **T3** spread of medium winding's pitch vs tight and loose (MAD, permutation within visibility quintiles): INSUFFICIENT / NOT WIDER / WIDER / NARROWER. **T4** ordering (Kendall τ within visibility quintiles, 2,000 permutations): NOT ORDERED / REVERSED / ORDERED BEYOND VISIBILITY (τ_within ≥ 0.5 τ_raw) / ORDERED, MOSTLY VISIBILITY; and U3's winding axis vs pitch: NONE / REVERSED UNDER CONTROL / MOSTLY VISIBILITY (retention < 0.5) / NOT ABOVE UNTRAINED / WINDING BEYOND VISIBILITY. **T5** exploratory, no states.

Table built mechanically: one row per state-bearing field of the record (path · field), the state per encoder, and — on the first state row of each path — the numeric fields recorded alongside it at the same level. "absent" = the path does not exist in that encoder's record. The path component `trained` is the record's fixed key for the encoder under test.

### E19 — Z3: B/T 2×2 with Y3's design (`artifacts/z3_bt.py --planted / --z3`)
What it tests: Simard B/T vs the GZ2 bulge-prominence average (reach ≥ 21), each leg controlling the other decoder's prediction plus visibility and required to beat the matched shared-quantity null; read by Y3's `state_2x2` (BOTH / MEASUREMENT BEYOND VOTES / VOTES BEYOND MEASUREMENT / SHARED ONLY / NEITHER / INVERTED); primary rows, unflagged rows as the sensitivity; `flag_sensitive` if they differ. D28 plants: BOTH, SHARED ONLY and NEITHER planted on the primary rows (keys `T2_both`, `T2_shared`, `T2_neither`), plus a calibration of the shared null's false-call rate.

Table built mechanically: one row per state-bearing field of the record (path · field), the state per encoder, and — on the first state row of each path — the numeric fields recorded alongside it at the same level. "absent" = the path does not exist in that encoder's record. The path component `trained` is the record's fixed key for the encoder under test.

### E20 — AA1: label efficiency (`artifacts/aa1_label_efficiency.py --planted / --aa1`)
What it tests: the ladder's probe (C = 1) trained on n ∈ {100, 300, 1k, 3k, 10k, 30k, full = 39,997} galaxies (5 stratified subsets per n, the same subsets for every encoder), always scored on all test galaxies, for the trained encoder and three untrained draws; margin = trained − mean untrained AUC per subset, with SE over subsets. Per-answer state (`margin_state`, first match): INSUFFICIENT (< 3 n values) → NOT ABOVE UNTRAINED (no n with margin − 1.96 SE > 0) → LARGEST AT SMALL N / LARGEST AT LARGE N (the best margin at n ≤ 1,000 vs n > 1,000 differs by more than 2·√(SE₁² + SE₂²)) → FLAT. Catalogue state over evaluable answers: SUPPORTED (> 50% LARGEST AT SMALL N) / CONTRADICTED (> 50% LARGEST AT LARGE N) / MIXED / NOT EVALUABLE. The supervised arm is not implemented. Cell: **state**; margin at n = 100 / 1,000 / full (± SE); trained AUC at 100 / full; n90 / n95 (above chance).

### E22 — AA3a: pose code, instrumental or structural (`artifacts/aa3a_pose.py --planted / --aa3a`)
What it tests: on X1c's 2,000 galaxies, per-band light and core centroids; candidates INTER (between-band offsets) and COMMON (band-median core-minus-centroid). PC1/PC2 5-fold CV R² on each candidate set (1,000 permutations, BY). State (first match): a set "holds" if R² ≥ 0.05 and significant for both PCs; neither holds → **NEITHER**; both → BOTH / INSTRUMENTAL / STRUCTURAL / INSEPARABLE by whether the joint R² adds ≥ 0.02; only INTER → **INSTRUMENTAL**; only COMMON → **STRUCTURAL**. Causal arm: g-band shifts of 0.5 and 1 px in x and y, re-embedded; **RESPONDS** if the 1-px shift moves the paired PC by |Δ| ≥ 0.10 SD and > 1.96 SE on both axes, **RESPONDS ON ONE AXIS**, else **DOES NOT RESPOND**.

Table built mechanically: one row per state-bearing field of the record (path · field), the state per encoder, and — on the first state row of each path — the numeric fields recorded alongside it at the same level. "absent" = the path does not exist in that encoder's record. The path component `trained` is the record's fixed key for the encoder under test.

|---|---|---|---|---|

### E23 — AA3b: dihedral pose averaging (`artifacts/aa3b_pose_average.py --bank / --planted / --aa3b`)
What it tests: every union galaxy embedded under the 8 dihedral transforms and averaged; first a collapse check (the averaged PC1 and PC2 variance must each fall to ≤ 0.10 of the original, else the verdict is **NOT INTERPRETABLE**); then, averaged vs as-trained: **existence** (ladder probe AUC, paired bootstrap; **IMPROVED** if ΔAUC ≥ 0.01 with CI > 0, **WORSENED** if ≤ −0.01 with CI < 0, else **UNCHANGED**), **salience** (V3's held-out recovery, rank of verdict up/down) and **winding** (Y3's T4b axis vs Hayes pitch). Catalogue verdict (first match): not collapsed → NOT INTERPRETABLE; ≥ 2 better and ≥ 2 worse → MIXED; ≥ 2 better → POSE WAS COSTING; ≥ 2 worse → POSE WAS HELPING; else HARMLESS.

| item | A1 | A2 | A3 | agreement |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **verdict** | **NOT INTERPRETABLE**; existence improved 32, worsened 0; salience up 10, down 0 | **NOT INTERPRETABLE**; existence improved 23, worsened 0; salience up 0, down 1 | **NOT INTERPRETABLE**; existence improved 28, worsened 0; salience up 3, down 1 | 3/3 |

### E24 — DD interpretability chain (per encoder; `dd_sae_score.py`, `dd_sae.py`, `dd_part4b.py`, `dd_part1.py`, `dd_part1b.py`)
What it tests (tool validation): TopK SAEs (k = 32) at blocks {11, 6} × widths {8×, 16×}, trained on each encoder's tokens and on the shared untrained tokens (built once on A1, linked for A2/A3). **S3 faithfulness**: AUC drop of the 37 probes when tokens are replaced by their reconstruction; **PASS** (mean drop ≤ 0.02 and no powered answer > 0.05) > **UNEVEN** (mean ≤ 0.02, a powered answer > 0.05; powered = smaller class ≥ 100 and baseline AUC ≥ 0.6) > **FAIL** (mean > 0.02). The chosen width is 8× at block 11 unless 8× FAILs and 16× does not. **S1 offset latent**: a latent whose galaxy-level activation has |ρ| ≥ 0.5 with a band offset (g−r x/y, i−r x/y), not matched (|ρ| ≥ 0.2) by its nearest untrained latent: INSUFFICIENT (< 500) > FAIL (no candidate) > NOT-SPECIFIC > PASS; the offsets are v1's recorded offsets by design (inventory §5). **S2**: a latent firing on satellite-trail patches with precision ≥ 0.8 and recall ≥ 0.2 → DETECTED, else NOT DETECTED. **Part 4b**: 50 energy-matched random latent sets against the offset set; **INAPPLICABLE** when S1 found no candidate latent (nothing to remove). **V3′ (Part 1b)**: ring-stratified AUC of token-drop maps on GZ3D galaxies (bar, spiral) against untrained and brightness comparators, paired ΔAUC with bootstrap CI of the median: INSUFFICIENT (< 30) > REVERSED (a CI wholly below 0) > FAIL (a CI reaches 0) > WEAK (a median Δ < 0.02) > PASS. In the DD records the prefix `m_` and the field `auc_m` denote the encoder under test. D28 plants: `plants.json` (S1–S3), `part4b_plants.json`, `v3b_plants.json`.

Width note (as found in the code and records): `dd_part4b.py` reads the 8× SAE and its `_offset_set` refuses a record whose chosen width differs; for A1 the chosen width is 16× (S1 was therefore computed on 16×), and the `pools`/`plants`/`run` modes return INAPPLICABLE from S1's block-11 record before that check is reached. The `flags` and `stats` modes ran on the 8× SAE for all three encoders (`flags.npz`, `part4b_stats.npz`).

### E26 — Plate export (Almagest) (`artifacts/plate_export.py subset / export / sanity`)
What it builds: `plate.bin` (format v2) from the E1 bank: a 40,000-galaxy MiniBatchKMeans(400) subset with rare-class top-ups, 37 `_fit` probes on the full train split, a basis of the 37 QR-orthogonalised probe directions plus residual PCs, UMAP and 6-NN, raw GZ2 fractions/counts, `modelMag_r`, `expAB_r`, ra/dec. Criterion (sanity): every answer's AUC from the quantised (u8) readout must lie within the recorded target |Δ| of the float reference; the state below ("within target" / "over target") is read from the record's `answers_over_target` list (empty = within target).

## Technical failures

None. `state.json`: 205/205 entries `done`, exit 0; no entry `failed` or `skipped(dep)` (audit §1, §6). Operational notes (from `audit.md` §5–6 and `run_log.md`):

- **Manual pause, 14 Oct** (session 1, before the pause mechanism existed, hence no `pauses` record): cut short `E16.w2_measure.a1`; rerun 14 Oct 17:23:55 → `done`, 33.8 min.
- **Host reboot (kernel panic), 15 Oct 21:15**: cut short `E23.aa3b_aa3b.a2`; rerun 21:36:55 → `done`, 21.7 min.
- **Memory-guard pause, 15 Oct 22:18:44**: interrupted `E24.dd_train_trained_11_8.a2` after 15.4 min (`run_log.md` row `interrupted (paused)`); resumed 23:27:01, the entry rerun 23:27:52 → `done`, 14.4 min.
- **Second host reboot, 15 Oct 22:22**: fell inside the pause; nothing was running, nothing to rerun.
- The run finished 17 Oct 04:03:49 (`finished — {'done': 205}`). `run_log.md` has 206 rows: 205 final rows plus the one `interrupted (paused)` row.
- The runner records no SHA-1 for an interrupted attempt; every rerun output post-dates its rerun start, so each recorded SHA-1 is of the rerun's bytes, and no interrupted attempt left a file behind (audit §6).
- By design, not defects: the 7 `link` entries have no entry log (the runner logs a link only on failure), and the 3 `E24.dd_4b_pools.*` entries record no outputs (that mode reports pool sizes to its log only).

## Not implemented

As listed in `manifest.yaml` (`not_implemented`, `excluded`) and the brief:

- the 8×8 ablation (D11);
- the untrained-MLP bar (D25) — so R3 is unreachable and every existence-failing answer is R4 "MLP decode unadjudicated";
- the Tracy–Widom edge (the package path raises);
- the vote-floor sweep [1, 5, 11, 21, 37];
- inclination conditioning against b/a (only the E8/S1 and E14/V2 proxies exist);
- confound 2, recoverability vs imaging depth (no test exists);
- Scheme 2 as a 10-family ladder (`GradedExistenceTestUndecided`; only U3's 4 axes exist, E12);
- existence as specified ("max of four nulls"): the implemented existence test is D23 `untrained_z` against the K = 30 bank;
- nuisance matching as "triggered": matching always runs on the worst nuisance;
- the package's own uncertainty geometry is off; U2 (E11) replaces it;
- free probe replication (1B);
- confidence-as-target (D9);
- GalaxyMNIST;
- the supervised label-efficiency arm (AA1, E20, has only the self-supervised and untrained arms);
- E27 (package battery): excluded by decision (decision 3), not implemented as a ladder of record;
- follow-ups whose targets were chosen from another encoder's results, excluded by decision 4: E9 (T2), E13 (V1's suppression list), U2's hard-coded flagged list, `x1_vector_posthoc`, `dd_part4` / `dd_l2336_scan`. In their place, drivers whose targets were per-encoder derive them mechanically from that encoder's own upstream output: U2 (E11) and V3 (E15) from its own `t1_ladder.json` R1/R2 answers; r2/r1 on matched rows (E7) from its own r0;
- also outside this ladder by the manifest: E21 (AA2, Y1/Y2, Z1, N1/N2, J/K/L/O3, leakage, BB — encoder-free or one-off), E25 (the cross-encoder aligned criteria, a separate later step), and aa3b `--ensemble`, `dd_sae_cards`, DD V1/V2/V4/S2/Part 2;
- the 3 `E24.dd_4b_pools.*` entries ran (`done`) but record no outputs, so nothing is reported for them.
